`Mawile Name`: They knew that, in the distant future, Dark Matter would return.

## Chapter 21: Dark Matter

### Cutscene 229

`Ampharos Normal`

`Ampharos Name`: Let me start off by telling you about Dark Matter.

`Ampharos Name`: The truth is this:

`Ampharos Name`: Dark Matter is just one big bundle of hate, unhappiness, and pain...

`Ampharos Name`: It comes from all the Pokémon in the world.

`Partner Name`: ❓

`Partner Name`: It's...just hate and things?

`Ampharos Normal`

`Ampharos Name`: Yes.

`Ampharos Name`: Every time you feel annoyed with someone, every time you feel jealous of someone...

`Ampharos Name`: every negative feeling that any of us have...

`Ampharos Name`: all come together and grow...

`Ampharos Name`: and they form what is now attacking the Tree of Life.
